            Through the Evolution of Our Recycling-Oriented
       Business Model, We Will Contribute to the Realization
           of a True Resource-Recycling Society and Achieve
              Sustainable Enhancement of Corporate Value.

                                                  SEKIGUCHI Akira
                                             President and Representative Director




                             Corporate Culture that Should Be Preserved

     The origin of the DOWA Group can be traced back                   Since the mining business is untenable without
     to 1884, when Fujita Gumi, founded by Fujita                   coexistence with the environment and local
     Denzaburo, received a grant from the Japanese                  community, we have maintained a good, honest
     government for the Kosaka mine in Akita Prefecture,            attitude toward our partners, including the environ-
     and began mining operations there. The Kosaka                  ment and regional communities. Inheriting the
     mine later developed into one of Japan’s leading               will of our predecessors, who worked to deepen
     copper mines, contributing to the development of               our relationship with the community through the
     modern Japanese industry. The mining business                  development of social infrastructure such as
     deals with nature, something always beyond the                 electricity, water supply, schools, and hospitals,
     control of humans. Any deception through small                 in addition to preventing environmental destruc-
     tricks will undoubtedly result in painful repercus-            tion around the mine, we continue to deepen our
     sions. Therefore, at the heart of our Company is               relationship of trust with the community in
     a corporate culture that continues to hone our                 various ways.
     skills, face nature and materials head-on, and                    The DOWA Group has continued its 140-year
     conduct good, honest business with our custom-                 history not by pursuing short-term profits but by
     ers. We place the utmost importance on strict                  taking a long-term perspective, or as we would
     quality control through close communication with               say today, sustainability management, since its
     our customers. Once we lose credibility regarding              establishment. This corporate culture, connected
     product quality, the relationship we have built                to the business run by our ancestors, will con-
     with our customers over several decades will dis-              tinue to be the foundation for the sustainable
     appear instantly.                                              growth of the Group’s corporate value.

                                                                                                                                                               The DOWA Group’s Corporate Mission,
                                                                                                                                                                Vision, Values, and Code of Conduct


            DOWA was founded in 1884 as a mining and smelting company working with nonferrous metals. In                                                                            The reason for the existence of the DOWA Group
            1898, the Company developed technology for refining Kuroko (black ore), a complex sulfide ore that                                    Mission                         Contribute to creating an affluent, recycling-oriented
                                                                                                                                                                                  society through our business activities worldwide.
            was difficult to treat with the technology available at the time. This breakthrough set DOWA on a
            path toward the effective use of nonferrous metal resources, a path it still walks today. Over our
            more than 130-year history, we have remained committed to facing challenges head-on and con-
                                                                                                                                                                                    The vision of the DOWA Group (our goal in 2030)
            tinuing to refine our technology. During that time, we have also learned that the true key to resolv-                                                                 Contribute continuously to building a sustainable future
            ing social issues is to be mindful of the environment and to coexist with society, in addition to the
            pursuit of profit.
                                                                                                                                                   Vision                         through our core businesses, the promotion of resource
                                                                                                                                                                                  recycling, and the provision of excellent materials and
                                                                                                                                                                                  technologies.

     DOWA’s History
                                                                                                                                                                     Since its founding in 1884, the DOWA Group has worked in the field of basic materials to support the economic
                                                                                                                                                                     development of Japan. Over the course of its more than 130-year history, the Group has transformed its business
                                                                                                                                                                     and improved and developed its corporate structure while being subject to the variety of business conditions that
     Adapting to Change                                                                                                                                              come with the changing times. Therefore, it is safe to say that our ability to adapt to change, developed over many
                                                                                                                                                                     years, is a characteristic of the DOWA Group.

     DOWA’s Strengths




     03
                                                                                                                                                     The greatest strength of the DOWA Group is its unique recycling-oriented business model that combines its five core
     S T REN G T H                                                                                                                                   businesses.
                                                                                                                                                        We offer a wide range of products and services, from materials to parts, with a wide range of applications. These products
                                          Recycling-Oriented Business Model                                                                          and services are developed by leveraging our unique and advanced technological capabilities, earning us top market shares
                                                                                                                                                     and high profitability. In addition, by providing products and services to a wide range of markets, we have created a highly
                                                                                                                                                     diverse and stable profit structure that is less susceptible to trends in any one market.

     Value Creation Process


     The DOWA Group believes that its most important missions are to engage all of its stakeholders in a sincere and open
     manner through business operations emphasizing society and the environment and to contribute to the realization of                                      Realization of                                 Contribute to creating an affluent, recycling-oriented
     a sustainable society.                                                                                                                              Our Corporate Mission                                society through our business activities worldwide
       Looking at the different social issues that surround the Company, we aim to seek out opportunities by leveraging
     our value creation driver, which is centered on our recycling-oriented business model. At the same time, we intend to
     enhance sustainability management as a mechanism to reduce risk and ensure that we can continue to create value
     over the medium to long term. With these actions, we are realizing our Corporate Mission, “Contribute to creating an
                                                                                                                                                                                                  Achievement of Our Vision (Our Goal in 2030)
     affluent, recycling-oriented society through our business activities worldwide,” and our Vision (our goal in 2030).
                                                                                                                                                                   Contribute continuously to building a sustainable future through our core businesses,
                                                                                                                                                               the promotion of resource recycling, and the provision of excellent materials and technologies

       Materiality 3        Responding to Climate Change

                                                                                                                                       Strategies
     Climate change is a serious social challenge facing all industries and people worldwide. In light of the
                                                                                                                                       In fiscal 2021, we conducted a scenario analysis targeting our domestic operations, which account for more than 95%
     current global warming situation, we aim to achieve carbon neutrality by 2050 per our Climate Change
                                                                                                                                       of the Group’s total GHG emissions, and used the results to compile a series of risks and opportunities resulting from
     Policy established in August 2021.

     Roadmap to Achieve Carbon Neutrality by 2050                                                                                                                                                                                                   Achievements
     The Group will make maximum use of existing technologies and systematically introduce new technologies to achieve
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    The Group’s greenhouse gas emissions are characterized by the fact that they are primarily of electricity-derived and
     carbon neutrality by 2050. In addition to energy conservation, renewable energy, fuel conversion, electrification, and
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    waste-derived, accounting for about 80% of total emissions. CO2 emissions are particularly affected by the incineration
     other measures, we will actively promote the in-house development of biomass fuels, ammonia burners, and other in-
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    of waste received from outside sources, which varies depending on the amount and composition of the waste.      house initiatives. In the future, we will encourage climate change countermeasures through multiple options, such as
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    can be challenging to control waste generated externally, we are working to prevent global warming by promoting
     considering the use of negative emission technologies that capture and store CO2.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    thermal recycling, in which the heat generated during incineration is used to generate electricity or steam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     our manufacturing sites strive to reduce CO2 emissions by switching to energy-efficient equipment and reducing

                                                                                                                      Introduction and operation of power management
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      owns six hydroelectric power plants in Akita Prefecture.        [Initiatives for the Achievement of a Carbon Neutral Society:                                                                                                                                                                                  Biomass Power

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      we generated 2,267 MWh of electricity through biomass power.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      Waste Heat Power Generation
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    	The Group generates waste heat at six sites, five in Japan and one overseas. We generated 87 GWh of waste heat
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      power in fiscal 2022. Waste heat power generation is a method that uses the heat from incinerating waste materials
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      and exhaust heat from furnaces to produce high-temperature, high-pressure steam that turns turbines to generate
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      electricity. Energy can be generated from waste, leading to the curtailment of fossil fuels.

      Materiality 4   Protecting the Environment




     Environmental Conservation Initiatives                                                                                                    Conserving Biodiversity during Mine Development
                                                                                                                                               We promote mineral exploration and development projects for zinc mines to ensure a long-term zinc supply. Since
     Atmosphere and Water Management                                                                                                           mine development and operations have a large impact on the natural environment and local communities, we give
                                                                                                                                               careful consideration to the ecosystem and the water environment. Even at overseas mines currently under develop-
     The DOWA Group’s impact on the atmosphere and water is greatly affected by the composition of waste received by
                                                                                                                                               ment, we are taking appropriate measures that begin from the mineral exploration stage onward in order to minimize
     the Environmental Management & Recycling Business. Therefore, we are working to reduce said impact by enhancing
                                                                                                                                               the impact on the environment, such as regular environmental monitoring and environmental assessments that
     our management systems at each business location and implementing standards that are more stringent than national
                                                                                                                                               include ecosystem surveys and water quality assessments.
     regulations.

                                                                                                                                                  In Kosaka, which since the Meiji era has lost a lot of forest due to the impact of exhaust
      NOx                         t         439           480           530            BOD                t           66        50       52       gasses and deforestation by the mining and smelting industry, progress in forestation has
                                                                                                                                                  been made with the planting of black locust and Japanese cedar trees after WWII. However,
      SOx                         t         163           124           150
                                                                                                                                                  while the black locust is highly fertile, it is a species of tree with a relatively short life span,

                                                                                                                                       Serious Incidents
     The Environment, Health, and Safety Management System (EHS-MS) is defined as the integration of our environmental                                                                   •	Safety audit
     management system (ISO 14001) and occupational health and safety management system (ISO 45001). We aim to
     cooperate in compliance with these ISO standards. Under the Midterm Plan 2024, we aim to build and operate this
     EHS-MS at all business sites and to have internal control over the Environment (E) and health and safety (HS) for the
     entire Group. To achieve our goals, we will strive to prevent accidents by improving the management level of each of
     our two management systems, the environment and health and safety, while utilizing them. In addition, in fiscal 2022,
     we prepared the EHS-MS Manual, which clarifies roles, responsibilities, and authorities between DOWA Holdings and
     operating companies. We structured the manual in line with ISO standards to realize an occupational health and
     safety management system based on risk management that can operate, be maintained, and undergo improvement.
     We are expanding this manual to the entire Group, referring to examples of prior sites that have already implemented
     this manual.

      Theme                             Policies and examples of initiatives

      Prevention of child labor         We only hire workers who are at least 18 years of age.

                                        Applications from applicants are considered as a prerequisite for employment, and applicants are
      Prevention of forced labor        notified of their acceptance after one or more rounds of employment screening. We always present
                                        working conditions before employment, and employment begins upon agreement with the applicant.

                                        The Company respects the right of employees to organize and bargain collectively as a means of
                                        realizing labor-management consultations. To realize the creation of a comfortable workplace and
      Communication between             the development of the Company, we hold regular labor-management meetings, central labor-
      labor and management              management conferences, and other dialogues at various levels with the labor union to explain
                                        matters related to management policies and business plans and to discuss other matters such as
                                        working hours management and matters that have a significant impact on employees.

                                        As part of our effort to prevent overwork and promote work-style reform, we have set a Group
      Prevention of overwork            policy of reducing total annual actual working hours to less than 2,000 hours. We are promoting
                                        monthly efforts to control and provide guidance on long working hours.

                                        We comply with the minimum wage set by Japan and each country worldwide to which our
      Living wage above                 employees are assigned. For our employees working outside of Japan, we look at the minimum
      minimum wage                      wage in each country. Still, above that, we set our wages to a livable amount based on the price
                                        level and lifestyle in each country.



         T OP I C S
         Establishment of Human Rights Response Preparation Team
         To further accelerate our human rights response efforts, we formed and kicked off a Human Rights Response
         preparation Team under the umbrella of the Sustainability Subcommittee in September 2023. The team will be
         headed by the General Manager of the Sustainability Committee and will work across the Group.

                                                                                                                                                       tion system management. This system of regulations serves to standardize regulations across the Group and clarify

                                                                                                                                                       the responsibilities, authority, and authorization procedures of each position. In addition, the Internal Audit

     Reduction of Cross-Shareholdings
                                                                                                                                              Remuneration for Directors and Audit & Supervisory Board Members
     The Company positions its cross-shareholdings as those that will enhance the Company’s corporate value to maintain
     and strengthen relationships with business partners and other parties and form a strong relationship of trust with the                   The remuneration system for directors comprises basic remuneration, which is a fixed amount; performance-based
     issuing company. For each stock, the Board of Directors makes a comprehensive judgment as to whether or not to                           remuneration, which considers the Group’s consolidated performance; and restricted stock remuneration. This system
     continue to hold the stock based on whether or not it meets the original purpose of holding the stock and whether or                     has been designed with input from the Remuneration Committee. It is based on objective factors such as the Group’s
     not the benefits and risks associated with holding the stock are commensurate with the cost of capital. The Board of                     consolidated performance, the stock price of the Company, and remuneration levels outside the Company. However,
     Directors periodically reviews the details of such judgments. If it is determined that continuing to hold stock will not                 this system does not apply to outside directors as they take on a supervisory role from an independent and objective
     enhance corporate value, we will sell the shares in order, considering the impact on the market.                                         perspective. Therefore, they are ineligible for any remuneration based on individual performance. As each auditor is
        In fiscal 2022, the Board of Directors examined the pros and cons of continuing to hold all listed shares held by the                 independent from the execution of business operations, only fixed remuneration is paid to each auditor. The amount
     Group at a meeting held on December 9, 2022. As a result, we have decided to sell all of the shares of one stock held                    of remuneration is determined through discussions among the auditors within the total remuneration approved by the
     by the Company and all of the shares of one stock held by DOWA Metals & Mining Co., Ltd.                                                 General Meeting of Shareholders.
